Open tournamnets as large as this one tend to draw players from all sections of the Magic community. Hence, you tend to see things you might not at other high-level events. Here are a few of the crazier concoctions that graced the tables here in Milwaukee. Metagame be damned!

#### GP Milwaukee: Copland TC 1.514

#####
